In A.A.Piskoppel’s article “Conflict situation from the position-role
point of view” concepts of situation and conflict situation are discussed,
meaning of the use of the conflict situation  concept in conflictological the-
ory and practice is analyzed and an understanding of conflict situation in
terms of a regulative model of conflict  based on the organization-activi-
ty approach is introduced. A positionrole  interpretation of structure and
function of a situation that is ultimately connected to a concept of activity po-
sition is presented. Thereby it is presupposed  that each activity position as
an ultimate embodiment of positionrole  view determines its own actuali-
ty characterized by a specific system of means and ways of managing its
object. A distinction between “cultural ” and “societal” positions as regards
to a situation in general and to a conflict  situation in particular is intro-
duced. In the language of situations the cause of a conflict is the fact that
parties of interaction are from the very beginning in different situations, and
it is impossible to solve the conflict without bringing them to one common
situation. Thus solving of a conflict is understood as a transformation of
different situations wherein the parties  find themselves into one com-
mon situatiom.
S.V.Sokolovskiy in his survey essay “Autoethnography and Anthropo-
logical Studies of Science” traces the conceptual and terminological histo-
ries of ‘autoethnography’, its devel-

“ΩΓαΣ≡α≥α”».
opment as a distinctive genre of anthropological  writing and as a special
research method. He delineates its place among similar autobiographical
writing strategies and methods and discusses the problems of reflexivity,
objectivity, representation, and memory , as well as methodological possi-
bilities of autoethnographical research .
Under a common title “On the problems  of ‘places of memory’ as under-
stood by P.Nora” texts of a discussion of ideas on which a grand cultural her-
itage project “France – memory” is based is published. The importance of
the discussion is due to the fact that nowadays there is a tendency to create
a new culture paradigm based on the concept of collective memory.The main
object of analysis is an introductory article by an initiator of the project
Pierre Nora to the publication of the project materials. Each participant of a
discussion proposed his interpretation of the “places of memory” concept and
an appraisal of its meaning for cultural heritage activity; besides that reflexive
appraisals of other participants have been made.
Finally, publication of materials to the
Glossary-Reader of thought-activity  system methodology concepts is
continued. This time concepts “Category ”  and “Square scheme” are pre-
sented.
